# OVMap benchmark completion v2 implementation plan

2026-10-26：逐场景自有/OVI主审211/312（1688组）；完整分类分数210场景/1680矩阵，见[CLASSIFICATION_SCORES210.md](../../paper/static_ovmap/benchmark_completion_v2/CLASSIFICATION_SCORES210.md)。旧200汇总原字节及修改时间归档，其余3559份旧路径及93份冻结文件不变，后续采用新3720份基线。已审核配对池仍共同82场景，补充1687行/49页及条件诊断84场景；公开47场景/45场景表格，Replica0/8。GPU0/GPU2继续后续队列；下文保留历史范围，完整任务未结项。存储恢复及失败成本限定见NFS_RECOVERY.md。

> **For agentic workers:** Use superpowers:executing-plans. Primary owns design, numerical implementation, data roles, runtime diagnosis and review. Only fully specified mechanical leaves may be delegated.

**Goal:** Execute the complete frozen v2 supplement contract, preserve real coverage and dependency failures, and publish independently traceable scientific results.

**Architecture:** An independent phase DAG consumes immutable public A data and a finite STRUCT→FG/LR/ATTR lineage. CPU calibration is independent of local model/data availability. Real legacy and all-owner standard pipelines share locked MV1000 outputs while retaining separate prediction/evaluation protocols. External adapters preserve each author's full method. A stdlib-only terminal path checks unchanged task identities before any scientific imports.

**Tech Stack:** Existing Python environments, NumPy, frozen PyTorch models, official instance evaluators, Markdown/TeX/PDF, normal Git research commits.

**Spec:** ../paper/static_ovmap/benchmark_completion_v2/prompt/00_给Codex的最终执行指令.md and companion 01–05/config/reference operators.

## Global constraints

- BASE `9bbe040570ecbaec854e6d8aa94ba82a3a1afa2a`; branch `research/ovmap-benchmark-completion-v2`.
- No new training/architecture, parent all/bind, force push, main merge, changed old artifacts or fabricated metrics. No old A gain gate.
- Explicit roots > locked task paths > finite source-binding lineage; path translation never mutates original signed JSON.
- MV_ATTN matched1000 seeds17/29; clean8V; same-input FrozenFCBase; fixed CAP10/gamma.025 remains final method.
- Calibration 1331 logical candidates; full original279 denominator; unsupported9 retained; old160/40 roles exposed.
- Legacy fixed-eligible26×4×2; standard all-owner expected320×4×2; four full external methods×320. Coverage derives from locked lists.
- Original one-GPU profile is historical; the2026-10-23 authorization permits GPU0/GPU2 with one scene per card and separate original GPU locks. Three scientific CPU scorers, BLAS1; reserve20GiB plus twice the active and next scene estimates.
- Stage states: DONE/BLOCKED_DEPENDENCY/BLOCKED_RESOURCE/FAILED_RUNTIME/NOT_APPLICABLE. Seven independent completion flags.
- Only affected jobs rerun after real repairs; one retry per identical job; completed content reused. Four necessary validation groups, no full-repo tests/install/upgrades.
- Every scientific table cell has continuous result→prediction→input/model/protocol provenance; units are fractions until display.
- C1 scientific scope then C2 receipt/HANDOFF only; full remote SHA and one actual new remote artifact checked. Actual post-publication terminal resume recorded OUT; no third commit.

## Actual fourth-scene scoring repair

The pinned OpenMask3D evaluator raised `UnboundLocalError: ar_current` when its first class had no GT. `score_compat.py` adds only the absent-class auxiliary AR NaN assignment in memory, with an AST proof that removing that assignment restores the entire original function. Pinned author bytes and long-running numerical producers remain unchanged. All24 existing real scene/method AP arrays were recomputed before/after and were identical, including undefined entries. The first4-scene recovery scored all8 frozen variants using only CPU in5.96s; the original failure log remains intact.

New CLI scoring invocations install this adapter. A separate, locked, single CPU/BLAS1 `score_recovery.py --watch` handles only the exact already-recorded auxiliary-AR failure while the original stream retains its loaded code. It launches no GPU jobs, does not run duplicate scene reconstruction, and has a maximum of2 attempts per unchanged freeze/GT/repair identity. The old streaming progress retains its historical failure; current scored receipts and review coverage report4 scenes. Ten relevant compatibility/I/O/G4 tests and four publication tests passed. Final scientific coverage, full external ports and primary audit remain incomplete.

## MaskClustering full Replica execution contract

Add task-owned `mask_clustering_full.py` and a bounded test file. The controller resolves exact local assets, reports concrete absent files before importing a model, and launches five original stages: frontend, graph clustering, three-scale CLIP visual features, original CLIP text extraction with48 names, and original semantic query. Auxiliary class-agnostic evaluation is replaced only by the common final evaluator; it does not feed predictions. GPU stages go through the existing `runtime.execute(gpu=True)` lock/quota/job receipts; query is CPU. The text stage is shared across all8 scenes and has a scene-independent job identity. No source or active numerical producer is changed.

Verify consumed pinned MaskClustering files against their exact Git blobs before execution. Use `configs/scannet.json` numeric values unchanged; only checkpoint/path/dataset-vocabulary fields may be supplied separately. Preserve author step10, full CropFormer mask generation, full graph and postprocessing, original CLIP-H14 LAION2B, three crop scales and representative masks, author text normalization, raw semantic query and confidence. A task workspace holds relative output paths; an injected dataset factory only changes I/O. A local checkpoint/cache path may replace the corresponding download location without changing model architecture/tag. Record the actual checkpoint and project-code identities.

Test the real original CPU query on explicitly synthetic cached inputs with overlapping masks and an empty representative slot; expected labels/masks/confidences are hand-defined. Test rejection of an altered numeric config before any worker, plus a no-assets preflight that produces exact missing paths. These checks do not claim a complete baseline or formal Replica G3. Replica GT conversion uses the unchanged pinned author prefix, the complete four-field tuple and exact FP32 PLY coordinate order, independently of Ours' bundles and training-role calibration. Preserve native confidence and overlapping/empty masks. Compare the full class-by-IoU AP array and AP/AP50/AP25 against the pinned Open3DIS Replica evaluator before accepting a first formal scene. Pool actual matches over the frozen8 scenes, excluding every synthetic connection test. Wire the driver into baselines without blocking other methods, and allow only a complete real pool to enter T1.
